# Ascendance 3.36.21 mechanics audit

This branch repairs the confirmed audit defects and implements the ten previously unsupported items. See IMPLEMENTED_RULES.md for reviewed/default rule definitions. Full effect certification remains incomplete. Production and staging were not mutated. All functional requests target a local server with a new temporary persistence directory and isolated browser contexts. No deployment changes are included.

## Reproduction

Node 24; npm ci; npx playwright install --with-deps chromium; npm run audit:mechanics. On this cloud machine use CHROMIUM_PATH=/usr/bin/chromium. npm test runs the new checked-in foundation simulator. npm run stability:33621 runs the full browser suite. These are explicit replacements; the original historical suites were not recovered. The strict effect coverage gate remains separate from scenario pass/fail results.

## Current execution

{"passed":183}

Every passing row certifies only its named assertions. XP thresholds are not ability activation coverage; resolver tests are not full card coverage; dispatcher observations are not passes. Exceptions from generic fixtures need reproduction in valid gameplay before they become confirmed card defects.

## Remaining coverage gaps

Ability effects at levels 1–16, every branch/trigger/expiry of all 323 cards, all equipment and loot effects, full Enemy AI and targeting matrix, all campaign quests, concurrent reaction timing, and adversarial synchronization remain unverified except for explicit named assertions above. The generic dispatcher sweep must not be substituted for those tests. Staging was not used for mutations. A new-task environment restore and GitHub-hosted workflow execution are not verified here.
